All entries in this section: unspecified.

| Date | Resource | Description |

| 2026‑11‑04 | [GUI Grounding for Computer-Use Agents: A Survey](https://doi.org/10.20944/preprints202611.0431.v1) | A survey of GUI grounding organizes methods by learning, inference, and target representation, and reviews datasets, benchmarks, metrics, and inference costs. |
| 2023‑10‑20 | [Vision-Based Mobile App GUI Testing: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.13518) | Surveys vision-based mobile app GUI testing across test generation, record-and-replay and testing frameworks, drawing on 271 papers including 92 vision-based studies. |

## Papers

| Date | Resource | Environments | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2026‑11‑04 | [MKD2World: A Typed Spatial MarkDown World Model For Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06544) | web, desktop | Introduces MKD2World, a desktop web world model that represents interface elements in Typed Spatial Markdown and predicts post-action states, supported by AtomBlock-WebUI annotations. |
| 2026‑11‑04 | [Visual Token Routing: Learning to keep tokens that matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06854) | unspecified | Introduces Visual Token Routing (VTR), which selects visual tokens from current and earlier screenshots to reduce computer-use agent inference cost while retaining action-relevant information. |
| 2026‑10‑31 | [UI-UG 2: Advancing Mobile UI Understanding and Generation with Fine-Grained Supervision and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00926) | unspecified | Introduces UI-UG 2, a mobile UI model trained for component understanding, GUI grounding, and structured interface generation with render-aware RL. |
| 2026‑10‑27 | [Learning to Look Beyond Screens: Transferable Visual Observation from GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37093) | unspecified | Introduces LBS, a vision-language model trained with GUI grounding trajectories and crop-and-inspect decisions to study transfer of active visual observation. |
| 2026‑10‑26 | [Chart the Invisible: Interactive Perception for Hidden-Target Exploration in GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35237) | unspecified | Introduces Hagasu, a scrollable-region perception model that helps GUI agents decide where to swipe to reveal hidden targets, especially on mobile screens. |
| 2026‑10‑24 | [PreView: Enhancing GUI Grounding with Preprocessed Views and Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33645) | unspecified | Introduces PreView, a screenshot preprocessor that adds cropped views and candidate cues to help existing GUI grounding models identify controls for clicks. |
| 2026‑10‑23 | [IGG: A Benchmark for Interactive GUI Grounding under Visibility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32536) | unspecified | Introduces Interactive GUI Grounding (IGG), a benchmark that tests whether agents can reveal hidden interface targets through interaction before clicking them. |
| 2026‑10‑23 | [WaitLens: Temporal VLM-Based Adaptive Waiting for Industrial GUI Testing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32482) | unspecified | Introduces WaitLens, a vision-language model trained on GUI loading trajectories to decide when testing agents can stop waiting for an interface to load. |
| 2026‑10‑22 | [AgentVQA: A Unified Benchmark for Agentic Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31494) | web | AgentVQA is an offline visual question-answering benchmark across five agent domains, including web agents and questions involving UI element location. |
| 2026‑10‑22 | [PointerBench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Fine-Grained GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31274) | unspecified | POINTERBENCH is a diagnostic GUI grounding suite covering spreadsheet sub-elements, document characters and targets across professional applications. |
| 2026‑10‑19 | [Focused Omni-Modal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26203) | android | FocusGUI learns step-specific weights over screenshot, audio, video and interaction-context channels, injecting a compact residual into a frozen decoder without pruning its inputs. |
| 2026‑10‑18 | [From Seeing to Reporting: A Theory of Reliable Visual Set Generation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25503) | unspecified | FSR models visual reporting and gives inference-time procedures for frozen vision-language models, including screen parsing and GUI target selection. |
| 2026‑10‑17 | [fxBench: Evaluating and Understanding Formula Suggestions in Spreadsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24729) | unspecified | Introduces fxBench, a benchmark of spreadsheet formula suggestions, and examines context selection, intent understanding and formula writing for editing assistance. |
| 2026‑10‑14 | [STP-GUI: Spatially-Aware Token Pruning for Efficient Web-GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20390) | web, android | Introduces STP-GUI, which selects spatially coherent screenshot windows conditioned on the instruction to reduce visual tokens while retaining useful interface structure. |
| 2026‑10‑13 | [MHVF: Training-Free GUI Grounding via Verification-Guided Multi-Hypothesis Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17792) | web | Introduces MHVF, a training-free grounding method that explores several candidate screen regions, verifies them in local views, and uses those checks to guide further search. |
| 2026‑10‑13 | [Towards Safe Autonomy for Computer Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18508) | unspecified | Presents SA-CUA, separating trusted and untrusted visual content using accessibility-derived masking rules and a controlled channel for verifying information before endorsement. |
| 2026‑10‑12 | [Seg2Click: Learning Pixel-Level Action Masks for GUI Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16234) | unspecified | Recasts GUI grounding as predicting a mask of valid click locations from bounding-box supervision, then derives a click point with a deterministic selection rule. |
| 2026‑10‑09 | [CaptchaAgent: Verifiable Visual Reasoning for CAPTCHA Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12866) | web | Presents CaptchaAgent, which builds an explicit visual representation of objects and relations, refines ambiguous regions, and checks counterfactual consistency before acting on interactive CAPTCHAs. |
| 2026‑10‑09 | [When Charts Deceive Web Agents: A Paired Benchmark for Misleading Visualization-Grounded Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12823) | web | Introduces MisVisAgentBench, a paired executable benchmark that changes chart evidence while holding web tasks fixed, to trace how misleading visualizations affect agent decisions and downstream actions. |
| 2026‑10‑05 | [PlaySuite: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Interactive Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07127) | unspecified | Introduces PlaySuite, a closed-loop video-game benchmark that evaluates interactive visual agents, including computer-use agents, over extended play. |
| 2026‑10‑04 | [Recurrent Latent Visual Search for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05185) | unspecified | Introduces ReLaViS, which performs recurrent visual search inside a grounding model by repeatedly querying screenshot tokens and feeding the resulting spatial evidence back into the model. Coarse-to-fine supervision is constructed from existing interface-element annotations. |
| 2026‑09‑25 | [OpenVAM: Open-World Visual Attention Modeling with VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31364) | web | Combines dense saliency prediction with explanations of attended elements, including webpage layouts; evaluates human-attention agreement and textual rationales on static inputs. |
| 2026‑09‑23 | [Invisible in Space, Visible in Time: Motion Vision CAPTCHA against GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27461) | web | MVCAP-Bench tests live browser CAPTCHAs whose foreground meaning emerges through motion against dynamic backgrounds. A foreground-only control separates temporal camouflage difficulty from answer format and browser interaction. |
| 2026‑09‑16 | [RankGround: Efficient High-Resolution GUI Grounding via Lightweight Reranker-Guided Crop Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18690) | unspecified | RankGround trains a lightweight reranker to select a promising screenshot crop before one VLM localization call. Ranking supervision uses containment and boundary-aware augmentation, with pointwise and listwise training stages. |
| 2026‑09‑15 | [When Agents See Differently: Exposing UI Desynchronization Threats in Mobile Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16732) | android | Investigates mismatches between what humans see on a mobile display and what agents receive through screenshots or accessibility metadata. An automated evaluation framework embeds instruction-agnostic perturbations into functional APK clones and compares static and dynamic agent deviations with human noticeability. |
| 2026‑09‑12 | [PriMobiBench: Characterizing Visual Privacy Leakage in VLM-Driven Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13873) | unspecified | PriMobiBench evaluates direct privacy leakage and user profiling from mobile GUI screenshot trajectories, supported by the MobiLeak trace dataset. It also tests masking sensitive, task-irrelevant interface elements before cloud processing and measures the resulting privacy and utility trade-off. |
| 2026‑09‑09 | [LLaDA-UI: Bringing Block-wise Diffusion to Vision-Language GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13287) | web, desktop | LLaDA-UI adapts block-wise diffusion generation to a vision-language GUI agent through multimodal pretraining and GUI supervised fine-tuning. It evaluates grounding and navigation across multiple platform settings. |
| 2026‑09‑09 | [TRACE: Trajectory-robust Admission with Evidence Ordering for Efficient GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10297) | unspecified | TRACE orders screenshot evidence for monotonic visual-token reduction using layout priors, instruction relevance and feature novelty. Spatial token reserves and incremental KV contraction aim to preserve useful evidence for future GUI targets without repeatedly encoding retired frames. |
| 2026‑09‑02 | [Beyond the Verdict: Evidence-Aligned Evaluation of Visual Prompt-Injection Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05535) | web | Introduces Mind2Web-Injection, a benchmark for evaluating web-agent guardrail decisions on instruction-screenshot pairs using evidence boxes and image-side counterfactuals. |
| 2026‑08‑25 | [Reflection with Action-Induced Visual Differences for Desktop GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24015) | desktop, windows | Introduces Evidence-First Reflection, which separates extraction of action-induced screen changes from judgment of whether an operation achieved its objective. It filters visual differences into relevant evidence before a desktop agent’s reflector decides how execution should continue. |
| 2026‑08‑22 | [GUI-Primitives: Diagnosing Spatial Reasoning Failures in Vision-Language GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21832) | unspecified | Introduces GUI-Primitives, a paired-instruction benchmark that changes a spatial relation while holding the screenshot and anchor fixed. Its diagnostics separate failure to localize either candidate from failure to choose the candidate satisfying the relation. |
| 2026‑08‑22 | [Lexical Coupling in GUI Element Grounding: Sentence Embeddings Track Labels across Mobile and Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21794) | web, cross-platform | Studies whether text-embedding GUI grounding scores reflect semantic matching or recovery of visible labels. Same-screen ranking comparisons with lexical baselines expose differences between deployable fusion, oracle selection, and performance on label-poor targets. |
| 2026‑08‑18 | [ComponentBench: Diagnosing Component-Level Failures in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18307) | web | ComponentBench evaluates short interactions with canonical web UI components using verified tasks and matched human reference trajectories. Its diagnostic pipeline compares observation/action representations and analyzes structural difficulty, failure patterns and interaction efficiency. |
| 2026‑08‑16 | [Improving LLM-based Autonomous Web Agents with Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27770) | web | Trains HTML-element relevance filters on Mind2Web trajectories and transfers them to WebArena to reduce irrelevant browser context. It also evaluates a zero-shot ColBERT retriever and documents baseline web-agent failure modes. |
| 2026‑08‑10 | [Hallucination-Free GUI Grounding via Regression-Free Layout-Aware Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09654) | web | This framework separates frozen-model instruction expansion from a trained layout-aware matcher that localizes GUI elements without coordinate regression. Binary text/icon supervision supports the matcher, with evaluations on GUI grounding and Mind2Web element-selection tasks. |
| 2026‑08‑09 | [CAP: A Scalable Benchmark for Evaluating Cross-Site Browser Agents with Complex Actions and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08392) | web | CAP builds cross-site browser workflows by decomposing websites into operation and perception requirements and recomposing them into tasks. Its judge-based evaluation diagnoses complex interactions and visual-perception failures across real websites. |
| 2026‑08‑09 | [Not an A11y: How Android Accessibility Exposes Mobile AI Agents to Indirect Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08939) | android | Demonstrates that unsanitized Android accessibility tree metadata exposes mobile GUI agents to stealthy indirect prompt injection attacks. |
| 2026‑08‑06 | [Routing Is Least Learnable Where It Is Most Valuable: Bounds on Representation Routing for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06171) | web | Measures the complementarity, cost and rerun variability of six browser observation modes on WebArena and VisualWebArena. Its routing experiments distinguish an oracle cost opportunity from policies that fail to robustly outperform a fixed observation mode. |
| 2026‑08‑04 | [GUI-Lens: Coarse-to-Fine Cropping for GUI Grounding with General-Purpose VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03270) | unspecified | GUI-Lens uses OCR and detected interface components as coordinate references while a general-purpose VLM selects progressively enlarged screenshot crops. | 2025‑08‑06 | [Uncertainty-Aware GUI Agent: Adaptive Perception through Component Recommendation and Human-in-the-Loop Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04025) | unspecified | RecAgent narrows attention to recommended UI components and requests user feedback when task intent or decisions remain uncertain. It introduces ComplexAction to evaluate specified single-step GUI actions in difficult interface scenarios. |
| 2025‑07‑31 | [Phi-Ground Tech Report: Advancing Perception in GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23779) | cross-platform | Reports a GUI-grounding model family and empirical design choices covering source data, perception training, and coordinate prediction. |
| 2025‑07‑22 | [Screen2AX: Vision-Based Approach for Automatic macOS Accessibility Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16704) | desktop, macos | Introduces Screen2AX, which creates hierarchical accessibility metadata from macOS screenshots, plus datasets and Screen2AX-Task for desktop-agent evaluation. |
| 2025‑07‑14 | [A Training-Free, Task-Agnostic Framework for Enhancing MLLM Performance on High-Resolution Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10202) | unspecified | Introduces Extract Candidate then Predict (ECP), a training-free two-stage method for high-resolution images, evaluated partly on 4K GUI grounding. |
| 2025‑07‑08 | [R-VLM: Region-Aware Vision Language Model for Precise GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05673) | web, cross-platform | R-VLM localizes GUI elements through zoomed-in region proposals and an intersection-over-union-aware training objective. It evaluates the grounding model on ScreenSpot and AgentStudio and studies its effect on navigation in AITW and Mind2Web. |
| 2025‑06‑17 | [FormGym: Doing Paperwork with Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14079) | unspecified | Introduces FormGym, a benchmark for filling image-only forms, and FieldFinder, a tool that helps LLMs locate where to place text on those forms. |
| 2025‑06‑12 | [DiMo-GUI: Advancing Test-time Scaling in GUI Grounding via Modality-Aware Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00008) | cross-platform | Separates textual and iconic UI elements and iteratively focuses visual regions to refine grounding without additional model training. |
| 2025‑06‑02 | [FormFactory: An Interactive Benchmarking Suite for Multimodal Form-Filling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01520) | web | Introduces FormFactory, an interactive web-based benchmark for agents filling online forms, with varied field formats and evaluation of instruction-to-field alignment. |
| 2025‑05‑30 | [Open CaptchaWorld: A Comprehensive Web-based Platform for Testing and Benchmarking Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24878) | web | Open CaptchaWorld evaluates multimodal agents on interactive browser-based CAPTCHA puzzles. Its benchmark distinguishes task success from the depth of visual reasoning and interaction needed to complete a puzzle. |
| 2025‑05‑29 | [Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23678) | unspecified | Introduces ViGoRL, a visual reasoning model trained to reference image coordinates and zoom during reasoning; its evaluations include GUI element localization. |
| 2025‑05‑11 | [Seed1.5-VL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07062) | unspecified | Reports Seed1.5-VL, a general vision-language model evaluated on GUI control and gameplay alongside visual understanding and reasoning tasks. |
| 2025‑05‑08 | [Do MLLMs Capture How Interfaces Guide User Behavior? A Benchmark for Multimodal UI/UX Design Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05026) | unspecified | Introduces WiserUI-Bench, pairing real UI variants from industry A/B tests with observed behavioral winners and expert explanations. It evaluates models’ ability to predict the more effective interface and explain its effect on user behavior. |
| 2025‑05‑01 | [Learning to Contextualize Web Pages for Enhanced Decision Making by LLM Agents](https://openreview.net/forum?id=3Gzz7ZQLiz) | web | Trains a separate language model to rewrite lengthy web page observations into focused descriptions for LLM web agents, keeping training targets that let several LLMs predict the correct next action. |
| 2025‑05‑01 | [Visual Test-time Scaling for GUI Agent Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.00684) | web | RegionFocus adds visual test-time scaling by zooming into selected screenshot regions and maintaining an image-based map of landmarks and actions. It evaluates this grounding procedure with UI-TARS and Qwen2.5-VL on ScreenSpot-Pro and WebVoyager. |
| 2025‑04‑23 | [PixelWeb: The First Web GUI Dataset with Pixel-Wise Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16419) | web | Introduces PixelWeb, a webpage GUI dataset with element images, contours, and pixel masks. Its annotation pipeline combines bitmap channel information with DOM-based visibility and stacking analysis to address occlusion and overlapping elements. |
| 2025‑04‑10 | [Kimi-VL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07491) | unspecified | Presents Kimi-VL, a general vision-language model evaluated on multi-turn agent tasks including OSWorld and on ScreenSpot-Pro alongside other vision-language tasks. |
| 2025‑03‑26 | [ScreenLLM: Stateful Screen Schema for Efficient Action Understanding and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20978) | cross-platform | Represents interface interactions with a stateful screen schema to model user behavior and support action understanding and prediction. |
| 2025‑03‑19 | [UI-Vision: A Desktop-centric GUI Benchmark for Visual Perception and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15661) | desktop | Provides annotated desktop demonstrations for evaluating element grounding, layout grounding, and action prediction. |
| 2025‑03‑18 | [MP-GUI: Modality Perception with MLLMs for GUI Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14021) | cross-platform | Uses specialized graphical, textual, and spatial perception components with a data-collection pipeline to improve GUI understanding. |
| 2025‑03‑18 | [WebNav: An Intelligent Agent for Voice-Controlled Web Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13843) | web | Introduces WebNav, a web navigation agent that turns natural-language commands into GUI actions using screenshot context, DOM labels, and separate LLMs for planning and action parameters. |
| 2025‑03‑14 | [DeskVision: Large Scale Desktop Region Captioning for Advanced GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11170) | desktop | Introduces AutoCaptioner, DeskVision, and GUIExplorer for generating desktop region descriptions and training interface-understanding models. |
| 2025‑03‑13 | [MIP against Agent: Malicious Image Patches Hijacking Multimodal OS Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10809) | desktop | Studies Malicious Image Patches (MIPs), altered screen regions that can steer screenshot-based OS agents into harmful mouse and keyboard actions. |
| 2025‑03‑05 | [SpiritSight Agent: Advanced GUI Agent with One Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03196) | cross-platform | Introduces SpiritSight and GUI-Lasagne data, combining multilevel GUI annotations with visual block parsing for high-resolution interface navigation. |
| 2025‑02‑19 | [Qwen2.5-VL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13923) | unspecified | Reports Qwen2.5-VL, a vision-language model that can act as a visual agent for tasks involving computer and mobile-device operation, alongside broader visual tasks. |
| 2025‑02‑12 | [TRISHUL: Towards Region Identification and Screen Hierarchy Understanding for Large VLM based GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08226) | cross-platform | Combines hierarchical screen parsing with spatially enriched element descriptions to support both locating GUI targets and describing indicated elements. |
| 2025‑02‑06 | [RWKV-UI: UI Understanding with Enhanced Perception and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03971) | web | RWKV-UI is a visual language model for high-resolution web interfaces that uses layout detection and reasoning-chain visual prompts to understand pages. |
| 2025 | [Are CAPTCHAs Still Bot-hard? Generalized Visual CAPTCHA Solving with Agentic Vision Language Model](https://www.usenix.org/conference/usenixsecurity25/presentation/teoh) | web | Presents Halligan, a VLM agent that abstracts visual CAPTCHAs into frames, elements and keypoints and solves each as a search problem through generated interaction code, tested on 2,600 challenges of 26 types. |
| 2024‑12‑13 | [Iris: Breaking GUI Complexity with Adaptive Focus and Self-Refining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10342) | web, desktop | Combines information-sensitive image cropping with a learning loop between element description and grounding for complex interface understanding. |
| 2024‑12‑12 | [Falcon-UI: Understanding GUI Before Following User Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09362) | web, android, cross-platform | Pretrains GUI-context understanding with instruction-free web interactions before fine-tuning Falcon-UI on instructed GUI tasks. |
| 2024‑12‑05 | [BigDocs: An Open Dataset for Training Multimodal Models on Document and Code Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04626) | unspecified | BigDocs-7.5M provides multimodal document data, while BigDocs-Bench includes GUI reasoning and image-to-code tasks relevant to screen understanding. |
| 2024‑10‑25 | [EDGE: Enhanced Grounded GUI Understanding with Enriched Multi-Granularity Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19461) | web, cross-platform | Synthesizes GUI training examples at multiple levels of detail from webpages and studies transfer of learned understanding to other interface environments. |
| 2024‑10‑24 | [Ferret-UI 2: Mastering Universal User Interface Understanding Across Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18967) | ios, android, web, cross-platform | Extends Ferret-UI across device platforms with adaptive visual scaling and synthetic data for referring, grounding, and user-centered UI tasks. |
| 2024‑10‑18 | [Test-Agent: A Multimodal App Automation Testing Framework Based on the Large Language Model](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/10778901) | harmonyos, cross-platform | Test-Agent uses an LLM, screenshots, and interface structure to generate and execute mobile app test actions from natural-language instructions across platforms. |
| 2024‑10‑17 | [Harnessing Webpage UIs for Text-Rich Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13824) | web | Builds MultiUI by generating instructions from webpage accessibility trees and pairing them with screenshots for multimodal training. Evaluation examines web-interface understanding, including VisualWebBench and Mind2Web, and transfer to other text-rich visual tasks. |
| 2024‑10‑04 | [SWE-bench Multimodal: Do AI Systems Generalize to Visual Software Domains?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03859) | web | Introduces SWE-bench Multimodal, issue-resolution tasks from 17 JavaScript libraries for interface design, diagramming, visualization and mapping, each with images, and tests how SWE-bench systems generalize to them. |
| 2024‑09‑30 | [DreamStruct: Understanding Slides and User Interfaces via Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00201) | cross-platform | Generates labeled slides and interface visuals through code to support visual element detection, description, and classification with less manual annotation. |
| 2024‑09‑30 | [MM1.5: Methods, Analysis & Insights from Multimodal LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.20566) | unspecified | MM1.5 is a multimodal model family trained with curated data mixtures; its MM1.5-UI variant targets mobile UI understanding. |
| 2024‑09‑26 | [Inferring Alt-text For UI Icons With Large Language Models During App Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18060) | unspecified | Introduces IconDesc, which generates mobile icon alt-text from partial UI context such as element identifiers, bounds, nearby text and hierarchy. Model evaluation and a user study examine the resulting accessibility descriptions. |
| 2024‑09‑23 | [MobileVLM: A Vision-Language Model for Better Intra- and Inter-UI Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14818) | unspecified | MobileVLM adds two pretraining stages and four UI tasks to learn element detail and relationships between successive screens. Its Mobile3M dataset contains three million Chinese mobile UI pages connected by recorded transition actions. |
| 2024‑09‑22 | [MobileViews: A Million-scale and Diverse Mobile GUI Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14337) | android | Builds mobile screenshot and view-hierarchy pairs through automated application traversal and evaluates their usefulness for GUI grounding. |
| 2024‑09‑18 | [The Impact of Element Ordering on LM Agent Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12089) | web, desktop | Studies how the ordering of UI elements in an agent observation affects web and desktop performance. A pixel-based element detector and dimensionality-reduction ordering are evaluated on OmniACT when document hierarchy is unavailable. |
| 2024‑09‑17 | [Grounded GUI Understanding for Vision-Based Spatial Intelligent Agent: Exemplified by Extended Reality Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10811) | unspecified | Orienter detects interactable GUI elements in XR applications by interpreting scene context and revisiting candidate elements through a validation and reflection loop. It addresses open-vocabulary element types and context-dependent interactability. |
| 2024‑09‑14 | [PeriGuru: A Peripheral Robotic Mobile App Operation Assistant based on GUI Image Understanding and Prompting with LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.09354) | unspecified | PeriGuru interprets smartphone GUI screenshots with computer vision and uses a language model to decide the next action. A peripheral robotic arm executes the phone interactions, providing an alternative mechanism for operating mobile apps. |
| 2024‑09‑06 | [WebQuest: A Benchmark for Multimodal QA on Web Page Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13711) | web | Introduces question answering over individual screens, multiple web pages, and navigation traces to test information synthesis across views. |
| 2024‑08‑01 | [OmniParser for Pure Vision Based GUI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00203) | cross-platform | Parses screenshots into interactable regions and semantic element descriptions to support visually grounded agent actions. |
| 2024‑07‑11 | [UICrit: Enhancing Automated Design Evaluation with a UICritique Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08850) | unspecified | Introduces designer-authored mobile UI critiques and quality ratings, studying how targeted examples improve automated design feedback. |
| 2024‑07‑05 | [MobileFlow: A Multimodal LLM For Mobile GUI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04346) | unspecified | Introduces a mobile GUI model using hybrid visual encoders, mixture-of-experts expansion, and alignment training for multilingual interfaces. |
| 2024‑07‑03 | [AMEX: Android Multi-annotation Expo Dataset for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.17490) | android | Introduces Android screenshots with element locations, functionality descriptions, and instructions linked to sequences of GUI actions. |
| 2024‑07‑03 | [Seeing is Believing: Vision-driven Non-crash Functional Bug Detection for Mobile Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03037) | unspecified | Trident combines exploration, monitoring, and detection agents to identify functional bugs in a mobile app. It reasons over screenshot sequences and interface transitions to construct test oracles, evaluated on 590 non-crash bugs and additional live apps. |
| 2024‑07 | [RealWeb: A Benchmark for Universal Instruction Following in Realistic Web Services Navigation](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/10707522) | web | Introduces RealWeb, a Chinese benchmark of 11,739 instructions on 40 real websites with annotated screenshots and slot trees, plus a baseline that detects page regions, parses instructions and acts on the pages. |
| 2024‑06‑27 | [Read Anywhere Pointed: Layout-aware GUI Screen Reading with Tree-of-Lens Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19263) | cross-platform | Introduces Screen Point-and-Read and a hierarchical layout approach that interprets screen regions indicated by a user-selected point. |
| 2024‑06‑20 | [Identifying User Goals from UI Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14314) | android, web, cross-platform | Defines goal identification from observed UI trajectories and an evaluation method for comparing intent descriptions within the same interface context. Experiments reuse Android and web automation data to compare human and model inference of users’ detailed goals. |
| 2024‑06‑20 | [VGA: Vision GUI Assistant -- Minimizing Hallucinations through Image-Centric Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14056) | cross-platform | Uses image-dependent question-answer data and staged fine-tuning to improve GUI comprehension and reduce unsupported visual answers. |
| 2024‑06‑19 | [GUI Action Narrator: Where and When Did That Action Take Place?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13719) | cross-platform | Introduces Act2Cap and a cursor-guided method for describing GUI actions from video, with temporal and spatial localization challenges. |
| 2024‑06‑17 | [GUICourse: From General Vision Language Models to Versatile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11317) | cross-platform | Introduces GUIEnv, GUIAct, and GUIChat datasets to develop OCR, grounding, component knowledge, and interaction abilities in visual-language models. |
| 2024‑06‑16 | [GUI-World: A Video Benchmark and Dataset for Multimodal GUI-oriented Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10819) | cross-platform | Introduces video-based GUI questions and annotations for studying temporal interface understanding across varied interaction settings. |
| 2024‑06‑12 | [Practical, Automated Scenario-based Mobile App Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08340) | unspecified | ScenTest builds scenario-specific event knowledge graphs from crowdsourced test reports containing GUI and action information. It uses the current app state and testing context to generate scenario tests across applications. |
| 2024‑06‑12 | [Tell Me What's Next: Textual Foresight for Generic UI Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07822) | unspecified | Textual Foresight learns UI representations by describing the future screen expected after an action on the current interface. The OpenApp dataset supports pretraining and downstream evaluation of representations that connect individual elements with whole-screen changes. |
| 2024‑06‑11 | [CAAP: Context-Aware Action Planning Prompting to Solve Computer Tasks with Front-End UI Only](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06947) | web | CAAP converts screenshots into textual observations and plans keyboard-and-mouse actions using a prompt that examines task context from multiple perspectives. The agent is evaluated on MiniWoB++ and WebShop using only front-end visual observations. |
| 2024‑06 | [Visual Grounding for User Interfaces](https://aclanthology.org/2024.naacl-industry.9/) | android | Proposes LVG, which detects and grounds the UI element named by a free-form command in an Android screenshot, using layout-guided contrastive learning and synthetic referring expressions generated from Rico screens. |
| 2024‑05‑24 | [V-Zen: Efficient GUI Understanding and Precise Grounding With A Novel Multimodal LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15341) | unspecified | Describes V-Zen, a multimodal GUI model with dual-resolution image encoders for grounding and next-action prediction, fine-tuned using GUIDE interface elements and task sequences. |
| 2024‑05‑21 | [SmartFlow: Robotic Process Automation using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12842) | unspecified | Presents SmartFlow, which converts visible GUI elements into text for language-model action planning and executes the plan through a scripting engine. A collection of enterprise applications evaluates adaptation to different layouts and tasks. |
| 2024‑05‑11 | [MUD: Towards a Large-Scale and Noise-Filtered UI Dataset for Modern Style UI Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.07090) | android | MUD combines LLM-guided Android app exploration, noise filtering and human annotation to collect modern UI screenshots. The study evaluates the resulting data for element detection and UI retrieval. |
| 2024‑04‑09 | [VisualWebBench: How Far Have Multimodal LLMs Evolved in Web Page Understanding and Grounding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05955) | web | Separately evaluates web-page reading, understanding, and element grounding across tasks drawn from real websites. |
| 2024‑04‑08 | [Ferret-UI: Grounded Mobile UI Understanding with Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05719) | unspecified | Adapts multimodal modeling to mobile interfaces through detailed screen crops, region annotations, and UI reasoning tasks. |
| 2024‑03‑29 | [Draw-and-Understand: Leveraging Visual Prompts to Enable MLLMs to Comprehend What You Want](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.20271) | unspecified | Introduces Draw-and-Understand and MDVP-Bench for understanding visual prompts such as points and boxes; its multi-domain data includes mobile and web screenshots. |
| 2024‑03‑29 | [ReALM: Reference Resolution As Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.20329) | unspecified | Frames conversational and on-screen reference resolution as language modeling, allowing textual representations of screen entities to support user-reference interpretation. Evaluation separately examines on-screen references against an existing resolver and language-model baselines. |
| 2024‑03‑15 | [Computer User Interface Understanding. A New Dataset and a Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10170) | desktop | Introduces desktop interaction videos and a framework combining synthetic examples with hierarchical contrastive learning for interface classification. |
| 2024‑03‑08 | [UI Semantic Group Detection: Grouping UI Elements with Similar Semantics in Mobile Graphical User Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.04984) | android, ios | UISCGD detects semantic groups of adjacent text and non-text UI elements. It learns from Android and iOS screenshots to support interface retrieval, UI-to-code structure and screen-reader accessibility. |
| 2024‑03‑05 | [Cradle: Empowering Foundation Agents Towards General Computer Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03186) | desktop | Uses screenshot observations, planning modules, and generated low-level keyboard and mouse control for general computer interaction. |
| 2024‑02‑21 | [Improving Language Understanding from Screenshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14073) | unspecified | Studies language understanding from text-rendered screenshots using joint image-patch and text prediction; a perception foundation relevant to screen-reading models. |
| 2024‑02‑19 | [CoCo-Agent: A Comprehensive Cognitive MLLM Agent for Smartphone GUI Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.11941) | android | Combines screenshots, layout information, and action history with a factored action predictor for smartphone automation. |
| 2024‑02‑07 | [ScreenAI: A Vision-Language Model for UI and Infographics Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04615) | cross-platform | Introduces ScreenAI and screen-annotation training that supports UI question answering, navigation, and summarization data generation. |
| 2024‑02‑07 | [UEyes: An Eye-Tracking Dataset across User Interface Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05202) | web, desktop | Introduces UEyes, an eye-tracking dataset of webpage, desktop, mobile, and poster interfaces, and analyzes how people view these different UI types. |
| 2024‑02‑06 | [Dual-View Visual Contextualization for Web Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04476) | web | Uses screenshot geometry and visual content to enrich HTML element representations for web navigation. Nearby elements contribute textual and visual context, with evaluation on cross-task, cross-website, and cross-domain Mind2Web splits. |
| 2023‑12‑18 | [VisionTasker: Mobile Task Automation Using Vision Based UI Understanding and LLM Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.11190) | android | VisionTasker converts screenshots into textual UI interpretations and presents one interface at a time to an LLM planner for action selection. It evaluates visual representations and execution of 147 tasks on an Android smartphone, including integration with programming by demonstration. |
| 2023‑12‑14 | [CogAgent: A Visual Language Model for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.08914) | desktop, android, web | Introduces a visual-language model with low- and high-resolution image encoders for screenshot understanding and GUI navigation. |
| 2023‑10‑24 | [Make LLM a Testing Expert: Bringing Human-like Interaction to Mobile GUI Testing via Functionality-aware Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.15780) | unspecified | Introduces GPTDroid for mobile GUI testing: it feeds app page information to an LLM, runs generated test scripts and uses feedback and memory to guide further exploration. |
| 2023‑10‑08 | [UReader: Universal OCR-free Visually-situated Language Understanding with Multimodal Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.05126) | web | Introduces UReader, which combines unified instruction tuning, auxiliary text-reading tasks and shape-adaptive image crops for OCR-free visual language understanding. Its evaluation includes webpage screenshots alongside documents, tables, charts and natural images. |
| 2023‑10‑07 | [ILuvUI: Instruction-tuned LangUage-Vision modeling of UIs from Machine Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.04869) | unspecified | ILuvUI generates 335,000 conversational training examples paired with UI screenshots, covering questions, descriptions, and plans. The resulting vision-language model is assessed on element detection, response quality, and examples of multi-step UI navigation. |
| 2023‑10‑03 | [AXNav: Replaying Accessibility Tests from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.02424) | unspecified | Introduces an LLM and pixel-based UI system that executes natural-language accessibility tests, records navigable videos and flags potential interface issues. |
| 2023‑08‑11 | [Unveiling the Tricks: Automated Detection of Dark Patterns in Mobile Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.05898) | unspecified | Introduces UIGuard, a system that examines mobile UI visuals and text to detect dark patterns, together with a labeled dataset of benign and malicious interfaces. |
| 2023‑05‑16 | [Mobile User Interface Element Detection Via Adaptively Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.09699) | unspecified | Introduces MUI-zh and APT for detecting mobile UI elements, combining visual features with OCR descriptions to adjust category prompts in CLIP-based detectors. |
| 2023‑04 | [Automating GUI-based Software Testing with GPT-3](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/10132233) | desktop, cross-platform | A GPT-3-based GUI testing method interprets natural-language test cases, tracks current interface elements in prompts, and navigates desktop and mobile applications. |
| 2023‑01‑30 | [WebUI: A Dataset for Enhancing Visual UI Understanding with Web Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.13280) | web | Collects rendered webpages and structural metadata for visual UI modeling, studying how web semantics transfer to interface-understanding tasks. |
| 2023‑01‑23 | [Lexi: Self-Supervised Learning of the UI Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.10165) | unspecified | Introduces Lexi and UICaption to learn UI image-text representations from descriptions of interface functionality. Evaluation covers UI action entailment, instruction-based retrieval, referring-expression grounding, and UI entity recognition. |
| 2022‑12‑09 | [Fill in the Blank: Context-aware Automated Text Input Generation for Mobile GUI Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.04732) | unspecified | Introduces QTypist, an LLM-based method that generates text inputs from mobile GUI context and integrates with automated testing tools to help them reach further app pages. |
| 2022‑10‑08 | [Understanding HTML with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.03945) | web | Studies language models for HTML element classification, page description and autonomous web navigation. Fine-tuned models are evaluated on MiniWoB, and a distilled, automatically labeled CommonCrawl HTML corpus supports the training approach. |
| 2022‑10‑07 | [Pix2Struct: Screenshot Parsing as Pretraining for Visual Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.03347) | web | Pix2Struct pretrains an image-to-text model by reconstructing simplified HTML from masked webpage screenshots. Its variable-resolution visual representation is evaluated across several domains, including user-interface understanding. |
| 2022‑10‑06 | [Towards Better Semantic Understanding of Mobile Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.02663) | unspecified | Augments RICO screens and view hierarchies with human labels for icon shape and meaning and for links between UI elements and text labels. It compares image-only and multimodal models on semantic understanding of previously unseen apps. |
| 2022‑09‑29 | [Spotlight: Mobile UI Understanding using Vision-Language Models with a Focus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.14927) | unspecified | Introduces Spotlight, a vision-language model conditioned on a UI screenshot and a selected region of interest. It evaluates screenshot-based mobile UI understanding and explores multitask learning and few-shot prompting. |
| 2022‑09‑18 | [Enabling Conversational Interaction with Mobile UI using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.08655) | unspecified | Studies few-shot prompting of a single language model for four mobile-UI interaction tasks, aiming to support conversational interface use without a separate trained model for each task. |
| 2022‑09‑16 | [ScreenQA: Large-Scale Question-Answer Pairs over Mobile App Screenshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.08199) | unspecified | Introduces screenshot question answering over mobile interfaces, linking answers to screen content and bounding boxes. |
| 2022‑01‑11 | [Learning to Denoise Raw Mobile UI Layouts for Improving Datasets at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2201.04100) | unspecified | Introduces CLAY, a pipeline that repairs noisy mobile UI layouts by removing nodes without valid visual counterparts and assigning semantic element types, supported by human-annotated layouts for evaluation. |
| 2021‑12‑10 | [VUT: Versatile UI Transformer for Multi-Modal Multi-Task User Interface Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2112.05692) | unspecified | The Versatile UI Transformer jointly models interface screenshots, structure, and language for five UI tasks. Its encoder supports element detection when structural metadata is absent, while an autoregressive component handles UI question answering and command grounding. |
| 2021‑08‑07 | [Screen2Words: Automatic Mobile UI Summarization with Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2108.03353) | unspecified | Studies mobile-screen summarization using visual, textual, and structural UI information, with a dataset of human-written descriptions. |
| 2021‑07‑29 | [UIBert: Learning Generic Multimodal Representations for UI Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2107.13731) | unspecified | Learns shared image, text, and structural UI representations through self-supervised tasks based on relationships among interface components. |
| 2021‑05‑25 | [Understanding Mobile GUI: from Pixel-Words to Screen-Sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2105.11941) | unspecified | Models mobile screens as atomic text and graphic components whose relationships are learned by a Screen Transformer. The work introduces RICO-PW annotations and evaluates metadata-free screen understanding on relation, clickability, retrieval, and app-classification tasks. |
| 2021‑01‑23 | [WebSRC: A Dataset for Web-Based Structural Reading Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2101.09465) | web | Introduces question answering over webpage text and structure, with aligned HTML, screenshots, and metadata for structural reading comprehension. |
| 2021‑01‑13 | [Screen Recognition: Creating Accessibility Metadata for Mobile Applications from Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2101.04893) | ios | Infers UI elements and accessibility metadata from mobile screenshots to support screen-reader interaction with iOS applications. |
| 2020‑12‑22 | [ActionBert: Leveraging User Actions for Semantic Understanding of User Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2012.12350) | unspecified | Pretrains ActionBert on user interaction traces to combine visual, linguistic, and UI-structure features. It evaluates the resulting UI representations on tasks including icon classification and description-based component retrieval. |
| 2020‑10‑08 | [Widget Captioning: Generating Natural Language Description for Mobile User Interface Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2010.04295) | unspecified | Introduces crowdsourced descriptions of mobile UI elements and studies multimodal models that generate useful widget captions. |
| 2020‑08‑12 | [Object Detection for Graphical User Interface: Old Fashioned or Deep Learning or a Combination?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2008.05132) | unspecified | Compares seven traditional and learned GUI element detectors on more than 50,000 interface images. The analysis motivates a coarse-to-fine non-text detector combined with a learned text detector to address GUI-specific localization requirements. |
| 2019‑02‑19 | [DOM-Q-NET: Grounded RL on Structured Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/1902.07257) | web | Introduces DOM-Q-NET, an RL architecture for web navigation that represents HTML as a tree and uses separate Q-function networks for clicking elements and typing text. |
| 2017‑10‑20 | [Rico: A Mobile App Dataset for Building Data-Driven Design Applications](https://doi.org/10.1145/3126594.3126651) | android | Introduces Rico, collecting Android interface designs and interaction traces through crowdsourced use followed by automated exploration. The paper demonstrates UI-layout retrieval using learned representations and describes visual, textual, structural, and interaction data. |
| 2009‑10 | [Sikuli: Using GUI screenshots for search and automation](https://doi.org/10.1145/1622176.1622213) | unspecified | Introduces screenshot-based GUI search and visual scripting, using image patterns to locate interface targets and direct mouse and keyboard events. It evaluates screenshot queries and demonstrates automation and interactive-help tasks. |
